# Skills · O · part 39

880 entries, openabstracts-citations to openai-reviewer. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **openabstracts-citations**: Find real academic papers and verify citations using the OpenAbstracts journal-search index (10M+ papers, 100,000+ journals). Use this whenever the user asks you to find a source, check whether a citation is real, fix a fabricated or vague reference, or needs a properly formatted citation for a claim. ([MohammedAlshami/openabstracts](https://github.com/MohammedAlshami/openabstracts/tree/HEAD/skills/openabstracts-citations))
- **openaca-candidate-review**: Annotate or re-review OpenACA seed candidates in `candidates/` according to the canonical rules. Use when the user asks to annotate, review, fix, or re-classify candidate YAML files produced by the deterministic seeder. ([open-agent-security/openaca](https://github.com/open-agent-security/openaca/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/openaca-candidate-review) · ★9 · 2 copies)
- **openacc**: OpenACC GPU offload on NVIDIA. Use whenever you write `#pragma acc parallel loop`, `acc kernels`, `copyin`/`copyout`, or check `-Minfo=accel`, on an NVHPC build for an NVIDIA GPU. ([spcl/HPCAgent-Bench](https://github.com/spcl/HPCAgent-Bench/tree/HEAD/hpcagent_bench/skills/openacc) · ★9)
- **openacc**: Template-based skill for automatically inserting OpenACC directives into Fortran code. Uses a verified pattern library to achieve reliable GPU parallelization. ([RIKEN-RCCS/HPC-Agentic-SDK](https://github.com/RIKEN-RCCS/HPC-Agentic-SDK/tree/HEAD/openacc/skills/openacc) · ★7)
- **openacc-3.4**: Authoritative knowledge base from the OpenACC Application Programming Interface v3.4 specification. CONSULT THIS BEFORE ANSWERING — do not answer OpenACC questions from memory; directive/clause semantics, data-clause behavior, and async-queue ordering rules are easy to misremember and version-sensitive. TRIGGER whenever a question concerns: writing/reading/debugging any OpenACC directive (#pragma acc / !$acc); offloading C/C++/Fortran to GPU or multicore; gang/worker/vector parallelism or execution modes; data clauses (copy/copyin/copyout/create/present/no_create/deviceptr/attach) or data-reg… ([szaghi/dotfiles](https://github.com/szaghi/dotfiles/tree/HEAD/claude/.claude/skills/openacc-3.4) · ★6)
- **openacc-porting**: Port or review this repository's 3D GPU solvers using the existing OpenACC style from `3DRBOpenacc.F90` and `3DRBOpenaccMpi.F90`. Use when adding `!$acc` directives, creating data regions, handling reductions, synchronizing device and host state, or translating CPU loops to GPU kernels without changing solver physics. ([19134984/-2D-3D-](https://github.com/19134984/-2D-3D-/tree/HEAD/2DRB/codex-skills/openacc-porting) · ★1)
- **openaccountants**: Use for questions about tax, VAT/GST, US sales tax, payroll or filing in any country or US state, for example "how much tax will I pay in Spain", "do I need to register for VAT", "when is my return due" or "how is a freelancer taxed in Germany". Uses the OpenAccountants server configured by this plugin. ([openaccountants/marketplace](https://github.com/openaccountants/marketplace/tree/HEAD/plugins/openaccountants/skills/openaccountants) · ★5)
